# Master calculation inventory (Opus × Codex merge)

Merged 2026-11-10 from the Opus inventory ([../CALCULATIONS-OPUS.md](../CALCULATIONS-OPUS.md)) and the Codex (Astra) inventory (notes-folder package, see [MERGE-NOTES.md](MERGE-NOTES.md)). Inventory only: **conflicts are recorded, not resolved, and leads are not audited.** Machine-readable: [master.json](master.json); ID mapping: [crosswalk.csv](crosswalk.csv); per-family audit packets in [packets/](packets/).

## Coverage

- Master entries: **1358** (Opus records 1029, Codex records 1318: 989 primary/supplemental records matched, the rest Codex-only).
- Found by both: **592**; Opus only: **437**; Codex only: **329** (258 primary calculation records, 71 supplemental symbol candidates).
- Matched pairs: 1283 (location 888, codex_part_of_opus 394, name+file 1), plus 138 low-confidence 'possible' links kept as cross-references only. Pairs are **mechanical** (file + symbol/line proximity, primary-file containment, or same-file name similarity) and unconfirmed by reading code. One Codex record can pair with several Opus entries and vice versa; master entries are anchored on Opus entries, with matched Codex records attached.
- Entries with conflicts: **3** (7 conflicts). Leads (Opus red flags + Codex unknowns): 3632.
